  "raw_model_response": "The transaction details show that Ashlee Martinez sent $38.00 to Spencer Powell for the dinner at Azure Harbor Bistro. ## Interpretation

- **VERIFIED:** task outcomes, actions, observations, and first divergences are from immutable raw rows.
- **INFERENCE:** N1-N2 differences are reported directionally; they are not presumed to be beneficial or memory-specific.
- **UNVERIFIED:** mechanisms downstream of the first recorded behavioral divergence.
